### F-010

<!-- finding F-010 key=064ff17859c364cf694d3427509ce44f486abc9faf758bc2c5d3b538a5df182c discovered=2026-11-12T02:58:47.000000Z source=2026-11-12 -->
<!-- first-error finding=F-010 at=2026-11-12T01:17:25.465911Z -->
<!-- job-time finding=F-010 job=130901758454 at=2026-11-12T01:17:25.465911Z -->

**关键原始错误**

```text
2026-11-12T01:17:25.4659113Z error: Request failed after 3 retries in 9.0s
2026-11-12T01:17:25.4660424Z   cause: Failed to fetch: https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu/msgfy/
2026-11-12T01:17:25.4661794Z   cause: HTTP status server error (503 Service Unavailable) for url (https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu/msgfy/)
```

**原因分析**

PR #21607 的 E2E-upstream 在共享 main 的 linux-aarch64-a2b3-1 安装步骤访问 https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu/msgfy/ 时重试 3 次后收到 HTTP 503，尚未运行测试。实际 pull_request merge workflow 为 ccd3d7f4140d632b39f6a34dd6daf3a9495e41e4，main 基线 8f191f1a4925011caf1febd29aaa7bfbb0281c9c 的同一 runner、镜像矩阵、uv pip install -r requirements-dev.txt 及两个 requirements 文件保持一致。精确比较显示 PR 仅新增 pull_request 触发、将 HF_HUB_OFFLINE 从 1 改为 0、修改后续测试清单；这些改动没有改变失败的 PyTorch 包索引或该安装命令。故本样本支持 main 共享依赖服务访问失败，不归 PR-only 环境。503 的服务端或中间代理深层原因未证实，也不推断所有 main CI 的健康状态。

```text
2026-11-12T02:39:07.8228095Z Failed to obtain the console log level. The possible causes are as follows:
```

```text
2026-11-12T02:54:34.8604733Z ##[error]Unable to process command '::add-matcher::/__w/_temp/mypy.json' successfully.
2026-11-12T02:54:34.8617206Z ##[error]Could not find file '/home/runner/_work/_temp/mypy.json'.
```

**原因分析**

main共享CPU runner在Prepare CI metadata成功复制matcher后，宿主注册mypy.json报文件不存在。PR #18491仅变更deepseek/权重传输源码及测试，精确merge/main父工作流逐字一致、复制及注册路径不变，支持共享容器/宿主matcher不可见模式；深层同步/存储原因未知，不证明main全局健康。

### F-129

<!-- finding F-129 key=aac205dc18b4b6f7d1d1f4f868c5d64d1326817ae60067e43cb1c374f2bb442f discovered=2026-11-12T21:06:36.294255Z source=2026-11-13 -->

**关键原始错误**

```text
2026-11-12T14:39:11.2352227Z install: cannot create regular file '/root/.cache/tests/run.sh': File exists
```

**原因分析**

main Nightly-A3在Prepare scripts把共享辅助脚本安装到/root/.cache/tests/run.sh时遭File exists，后续集群启动未运行。精确main工作流、checkout及nightly-ci-main-a3输入已核实，无PR代码覆盖。共享运行环境脚本安装失败已证实；是否并发竞争、残留文件或挂载实现所致仍未知，不声称共同底层根因。

**关键原始错误**

```text
Job 131030968122:
2026-11-12T07:10:42.3861972Z [network_error] https://gitcode.com/Ascend/msserviceprofiler/blob/master/ms_service_metric/README.md: TimeoutError: 
2026-11-12T07:10:57.4019870Z [network_error] https://gitcode.com/Ascend/msserviceprofiler/blob/master/ms_service_metric/README.md: TimeoutError: 
2026-11-12T07:12:57.5263740Z     Result: network_error - TimeoutError: 
2026-11-12T07:12:57.5264179Z     Sources: docs/source/user_guide/ms_service_metric.md
```

**关键原始错误**

```text
2026-11-12T02:52:30.9901464Z /usr/local/python3.12.16/lib/python3.12/site-packages/slipcover/__main__.py:119: UserWarning: Error reading in tests/coverage.json: [Errno 2] No such file or directory: 'tests/coverage.json'
2026-11-12T02:51:45.0734641Z /usr/local/python3.12.16/lib/python3.12/site-packages/slipcover/__main__.py:119: UserWarning: Error reading in tests/coverage.json: [Errno 2] No such file or directory: 'tests/coverage.json'
```

**原因分析**

PR #19280 在 _selected_tests.yaml 新增 Generate Cobertura XML 步骤，条件为 inputs.enable-coverage != true，却读取 tests/coverage.json。该步骤在未启用 coverage 时执行，两个 CPU Job 均由 slipcover 报文件不存在而退出；精确 releases/v0.30.0 基线没有此步骤。这是 PR 引入的 coverage 配置错误，不是 runner/容器基础设施故障。

- **首次报错：** 2026-11-12 10:51:45（上海）
- **已核实 Job：** 4 个
- **代表 Job：** https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-ascend/actions/runs/43141770333/job/130932509974

- https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-ascend/actions/runs/43141770333/job/130932509902 · 首次报错：2026-11-12 10:52:30（上海）

</details>

**本轮补充核实（保留以上历史证据）**

```text

2026-11-12T07:32:10.6804295Z /usr/local/python3.12.16/lib/python3.12/site-packages/slipcover/__main__.py:119: UserWarning: Error reading in tests/coverage.json: [Errno 2] No such file or directory: 'tests/coverage.json'
```

**原因分析**

PR #19280 两个 CPU Job 的新增 Generate Cobertura XML 步骤在 inputs.enable-coverage != true 时执行，却读取 tests/coverage.json，导致 slipcover 文件不存在错误。逐项日志确认执行 _selected_tests.yaml@refs/pull/19280/merge (c3aacdc24d91a9580b27ed8c7c49a55c80a4b5c3)；精确第一父目标基线 c741c57dbec21342e45c7091b88ada4284e4ee58 无此新增步骤，而 merge 第419-423行有该反向条件。这延续既有 F-016 配置错误，不新建模式；目标是 releases/v0.30.0，不冒充 main 当前健康结论。

**证据与基线**

- 逐项读取 Job 131034847726 的原始日志；Run 43173565551，head be22146e75983d01fa4973a6ba1bcaf4b59d1b1d，workflow .github/workflows/pr_test.yaml，event pull_request，实际 labels ['linux-amd64-cpu-8-hk']，失败步骤 Generate Cobertura XML from coverage data。
- 逐项读取 Job 131034847922 的原始日志；Run 43173565551，head be22146e75983d01fa4973a6ba1bcaf4b59d1b1d，workflow .github/workflows/pr_test.yaml，event pull_request，实际 labels ['linux-amd64-cpu-8-hk']，失败步骤 Generate Cobertura XML from coverage data。

```text
2026-11-12T06:12:53.4312432Z ##[error]tests/ut/models/test_engram_shared_pool.py:167: error: "set" of "Event" does not return a value (it only ever returns None)  [func-returns-value]
```

PR新增shared_pool测试使用Event.set返回值、未注解context及缺少Python3.12 ast.ClassDef type_params；Python3.12报告7错误，3.10/3.11各3错误。 精确执行workflow与main第一父相同；错误直接对应PR新增/修改代码。custom container implementation failed是非零退出包装，不是独立runner故障，不据此推断main全部健康。
